16/10/2025, 11:49 - Sahar✨: Hey, 

So I’ve looked into a few different authenticator apps, and Proton Authenticator seems to be the best option overall.

It’s fully cross-platform, works on Android, iPhone, Mac and Windows, and it has proper encrypted cloud backup and sync across all devices. That means the codes stay protected even if you lose a device. It’s also free, with no ads.

From what I’ve researched, there isn’t really any app that allows different users to have access to only certain 2FA codes (for example, some for you and others for us).

Also, setting up 2FA like this should help solve the location-based login issues we’ve been having, where it keeps asking for verification because I’m in the UK.

It all seems fairly straightforward: you’d just need to create a Proton account, and then I can log in and help set up all the accounts with 2FA.

For your personal accounts that you don’t want anyone else to have access to, you can simply make a separate Proton account and keep those there privately.

What do you think?

04/12/2025, 15:18 - Sahar✨: Greenvelope is a digital platform for sending Event Save the Dates and invitations. Save the Dates are used to announce the Event date and can also collect guest mailing addresses. However, Save the Dates do not support RSVP tracking.

If you choose to send full invitations through Greenvelope later, those can track RSVPs, plus-ones, meal selections, and other guest details.

*Information needed to use Greenvelope:*

Event details (date and location)

Guest names and email addresses (and phone numbers if sending by text)

Optional: guest mailing addresses (Greenvelope can collect these automatically)

*Key points:*

Save the Dates = announcement + optional address collection

Invitations = full RSVP and guest-tracking features
